Let's analyze LP method more: Could it alone reach target? Let's propose algorithm:
```
def lp_direction(a, M, solver='highs'):
    n=len(a)
    A = np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
    for i in range(n): A[i:i+n,i] = a
    res = linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=M*np.ones(2*n-1), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': ...})
    if success: g=res.x
```

```
Line search function:
```
def _line_search(a,h,MS,n,fs,deadline):
    best=a; bestMS=MS
    ts = [1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.15, 0.1, 0.075, 0.05, 0.03, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005]
    # To exploit convexity, we can eval all and choose min; but if deadline break.
    for t in ts:
       if time.deadline: break
       x = (1-t)*a + t*h
       x = _normalize(x)
       ms = _scaled_max(x,n,fs)
       if ms < bestMS: best=x; bestMS=ms
       # if t=1 and ms==best? continue
    return best,bestMS
```
Since F(t) convex, we can do better: evaluate at 1,0.5,0.25; if F(0.5)<M, do golden between 0 and1? But grid fine. To reduce evals, 10 points. For n=192, okay.

LP solve function:
```
def _lp_step(a, MS, n, fs, time_limit, hard_deadline):
    # build P
    af = n*a
    P = np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
    for i in range(n): P[i:i+n,i]=af
    nn=2*n-1
    A_ub = np.empty((nn,n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=P; A_ub[:,n] = -1.0
    b_ub = np.zeros(nn)
    c = np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0
    A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
    b_eq = np.ones(1)
    bounds = [(0,None)]*n + [(0,None)]
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
      res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    except TypeError: # old options?
      res = linprog(...)
    if res.x is not None and np.all(res.x[:-1] >= -1e-12):
       h = np.clip(res.x[:-1],0,None)
       if h.sum() <=0 or not finite: return None
       h /= h.sum()
       # compute mu = max(P@h)
       mu = float(np.max(P @ h))
       return h, mu
    else:
       return None
```
Could there be infeasibility due to P rows all zero? If n>0 and a nonneg sum1, P has some positive. Feasible.

Perturbation function:
```
def _perturb(a, rng, sigma):
   z = np.log(a + 1e-15)
   z = z + rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
   z = z - z.mean()
   # clip
   z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)
   b = np.exp(z - z.max()); if sum>0 return b/sum else normalize
```

Implement active LP solver:
```
def _active_lp(a, bS, MS, K, margin, n):
    L = 2*n-1
    if K <= 0 or K >= L:
       act = np.arange(L)
    else:
       # include top K and all with > MS - margin up to maybe 3*K? Let's keep simple top K plus threshold if count less.
       thresh = MS - margin
       mask = bS >= thresh
       cnt = mask.sum()
       if cnt <= K:
           act = np.flatnonzero(mask)
           if len(act) == 0: act = [0]
       else:
           # take top K (values)
           idx = np.argsort(bS[::-1]? Need top by value)
           # argsort reversing expensive but L small.
           act = np.argsort(bS)[::-1][:K]
       # ensure at least top 1
    m=len(act)
    A = np.zeros((m,n))
    for r,k in enumerate(act.astype(int)):
       s = max(0, k-n+1); e = min(n, k+1); idx=np.arange(s,e)
       A[r,s:e] = n*a[k-idx]
    c... A_ub hstack A and -1.
    solve
    h, u
    # check that h cross on active = max A@h (maybe u)
    u = max(A@h)
    return h,u,act
```

Let's consider implementing an "exact active-set Newton" for final polish:
Given a candidate a, identify active set A where b close to M. We want find a and active set such that there exists p on A and μ (Lagrange μ_sum) with:
- sum a=1, a_i≥0.
- For i with a_i>0, gradient: 2∑_{j∈A} p_j (a*a? derivative) = λ, i.e., 2 C(p,a)_i = λ.
- For active j, b_j = M (KKT with p_j≥0, complement nonactive b_j≤M).
This is a system: variables a, p, λ. Could solve via Newton? Nonlinear quadratic. Not simple. But maybe use dual pair from active LP: If u=MS, h support where cross min. Could update not.

Time management: We'll implement `run_optimizer(a, allowed_time, hard_deadline, rng)` that optimizes until slice ends. It returns best a and score. It can be called for multiple init. It preserves local best. At each iteration check time. If no improvement for several and time remaining, perturb.

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_sequence(a, deadline, slice_deadline, rng, start_MS=None, verbose=False):
    a = normalize
    best_a=a; best_MS=scaled_max
    cur=a; cur_MS=best_MS
    K=12; margin_frac=1e-5; no_imp=0; stuck=0
    tslice=deadline
    while time < tslice - 0.2:
       # if no improvement for many perturb
       bS = scaled_conv_values? We need b scaled to choose active. We can compute b full: b = conv(a,a); MS = n*max. Use bS = n*b for active threshold. For memory b length up to 2n.
       if MS < best_MS - 1e-13: best=...
       # active selection
       # maybe compute margin = max(1e-12, margin_frac*MS)
       h,u = active_lp(a, bS, MS, K, margin, n)
       if h is None: # increase K
          K = min(L, K*2+1); margin = max(margin*10, MS*1e-4); no_imp+=1; continue
       # line search
       new,newMS = line_search(cur, h, cur_MS, ...)
       if newMS < cur_MS - 1e-13:
           cur=new; cur_MS=newMS; no_imp=0
           # maybe reduce K if too large? Keep.
       else:
           no_imp+=1
           # expand active set
           K = min(L, max(K*2, 16))
           margin = max(margin*5, MS*1e-4)
           if no_imp >= 3:
               # try perturbation from best
               cur = perturb(best_a, rng, sigma=0.25)
               cur_MS = scaled_max(cur)
               K=16; margin=MS*1e-5; no_imp=1
           elif no_imp >= 8:
               # random larger
               cur = perturb(best_a, rng, sigma=0.6)
               cur_MS = ...
               K=32; margin=...
               no_imp=1
       # if improvement very tiny for many, maybe increase K?
       if cur_MS < best_MS - 1e-13:
           best=cur; best_MS=cur_MS
       if time < ...
    return best,best_MS

def _choose_active(bS, MS, K, margin, target_MS, L):
    thresh = max(MS - margin, target_MS - margin) if target_MS is not None else MS - margin
    # If target_MS > MS? maybe no.
    mask = bS >= thresh
    if mask.sum() <= K:
       act = np.flatnonzero(mask)
    else:
       # choose top K values, but ensure includes any > MS - margin? top K includes high.
       act = np.argsort(bS)[::-1][:K]
    # if act empty, act=[argmax]
    return act
```

Normalization:
```
def _as_prob(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if not finite or s<1e-300:
        return np.ones(arr.size)/arr.size
    return arr/s
```

Conv max and values:
```
def _conv_full(a):
    n=a.size
    if n <= 1024:
        return np.convolve(a,a)
    fs=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    F=rfft(a,fs)
    b=irfft(F*F,fs)
    return b[:2*n-1]
```
Numpy convolve may be slower for 1024 but okay. We can choose threshold 512. Let's use 768? To be safe with speed, use FFT for n>384? FFT rfft overhead maybe. For n=192 direct 37k fast. For n=512 direct 262k, 17 evals 4.5M per iter; if many iterations maybe. FFT 1024 length maybe 0.0002, direct 262k ~0.0002 too. We'll use direct for n<=512, FFT for >512. Need import next_fast_len.
```
def _conv_full(a, fs=None):
  n=a.size
  if n <= 512:
     return np.convolve(a,a)
  if fs is None: fs=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
  F=rfft(a,fs); return irfft(F*F,fs)[:2*n-1]
```
But if fs passed wrong? We'll precompute per job? Simpler no fs argument. Or cache fs in dict by n? Use `next_fast_len` once. Not critical.

Scaled max:
```
def _scaled_max(a):
   b=_conv_full(a)
   m=float(np.max(b))
   if not finite: return inf
   return float(a.size)*m
```
For active selection need scaled values: `bS = n * b`.

Parent initialization:
```
def _get_parent():
    h = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if h is None: return None
    arr=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
       s=arr.sum()
       if s>0: return arr/s
    return None
```
Need evaluate parent exact: `evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])`. Since arr sum1, exact same. But maybe parent raw cap? If raw values >1000? The preimported maybe not; but evaluate clips. We normalize for internal; score invariant if clipping not triggered? If raw has values >1000, clipping changes normalization and score. The evaluator applies min(1000). To be safe, when using parent raw, internal should be the post-clip normalized array: `p_post = np.clip(parent,0,1000); p=p_post/sum`. The history includes clipping before sum. The raw parent might have sum huge but no >1000 likely. We'll do post-clip.

Resample:
```
def _resample(parent, n):
   if parent is None: return None
   m=parent.size
   if m==n: return parent.copy()
   if m==1: return np.full(n, parent[0]/n)
   old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   y=np.interp(new, old, parent)
   y=np.maximum(y,0)
   s=y.sum(); if s<... uniform
   return y/s

   variants: uniform, triangular, parabolic, cos, exp, skew, two bumps, random, parent combos
```

Optimization function details:
```
def _optimize(a, deadline, rng, target_ms=TARGET_MS, min_time=1.0):
   n=a.size; a=_as_prob(a); best=a; best_ms=_scaled_max(a)
   cur=a.copy(); cur_ms=best_ms
   L=2*n-1
   K = 16 if n<=512 else 8? For t, use max(8, min(64, n//4))
   K = max(8, min(64, n//4))  # n=192 ->48? n//4=48. Maybe start 24: max(8,min(32,n//8?))
   # Let's set start 12, max 128.
   K = 12
   maxK = L if n<=1024 else 256
   margin_frac = 1e-5
   no_imp=0; iterations=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.3:
       # budget check
       b = _conv_full(cur)
       cur_ms = n * float(np.max(b))
       bS = n*b
       if cur_ms < best_ms - 1e-12:
           best=cur.copy(); best_ms=cur_ms
       # if very close to target and best good, maybe stop? Not.
       # choose active
       margin = max(1e-10, margin_frac*max(cur_ms, target_ms))
       thresh = max(cur_ms - margin, target_ms - margin)
       # active count?
       above = np.flatnonzero(bS >= thresh)
       if above.size ==0:
           above = np.array([int(np.argmax(bS))])
       cap = K
       if n <= 700 and (cur_ms - target_ms < 0.003 or no_imp>2):
           cap = L
       if above.size > cap:
           # choose top cap among above? If threshold includes many, top cap.
           above = above[np.argsort(bS[above])[::-1][:cap]]
       else:
           # If few above, ensure include top cap if cap > above? Maybe not.
           # But if above size < cap because threshold high, maybe expand K? Actually if threshold high at start, only near max. Good.
           pass
       act = above
       # maybe if act size < 4, add top few
       if act.size < 4:
           top = np.argsort(bS)[::-1][:min(4,L)]
           act = np.unique(np.concatenate([act, top]))
       # solve active LP
       h,u = _active_lp(cur, act, n)
       if h is None:
           K = min(L, K*2)
           margin *= 4
           no_imp +=1
           continue
       # Mix direction
       mix = 0.25 if u < cur_ms - 1e-8 else 0.0
       d1 = (1-mix)*h + mix*cur
       if mix>0 and not finite: d1=h
       d1 = _as_prob(d1) # maybe already
       new,new_ms = _line_search(cur, d1, cur_ms, deadline)
       if new_ms >= cur_ms - 1e-12 and mix>0:
           # try original
           new, new_ms = _line_search(cur, h, cur_ms, deadline)
       if new_ms < cur_ms - 1e-12:
           cur = new; cur_ms = new_ms; no_imp=0
           # maybe adjust K: if no_imp was high? no.
           # maybe if small improvement and K small, increase slowly?
       else:
           no_imp +=1
           K = min(L, max(K*2, 16))
           margin = max(margin*5, 1e-8)
           # If no improvement many times, perturb cur from best
           if no_imp >= 3:
               sigma = 0.2 + 0.1*(no_imp-3) #? cap
               cur = _perturb(best, rng, min(1.0, sigma))
               cur_ms = _scaled_max(cur)
               K = min(L, max(16, int(0.25*(2*n-1))))
               margin = max(1e-7, 1e-4*max(cur_ms,target_ms))
               no_imp = 1
           elif no_imp >= 10:
               # bigger random restart
               cur = _perturb(best, rng, 0.8)
               K = min(L, max(32, int(0.2*(2*n-1))))
               margin = ...; no_imp=1
       iterations +=1
       # If no_imp==0 and K>16? maybe reduce K to smaller for speed? Keep.
   return best,best_ms
```
Need `_active_lp` expects active indices and current a. It builds A and solves. It returns None if no feasible? Should always.

But in `_optimize`, we compute conv after update again. Could reuse from line search last? Not necessary.

Line search:
```
def _line_search(a, d, cur_ms, deadline):
   n=a.size
   best_ms=cur_ms; best=a
   ts = [1.0,0.8,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.3,0.25,0.2,0.15,0.1,0.075,0.05,0.03,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]
   # Maybe if cur_ms close best, include 0.0005? ok.
   for t in ts:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.5: break
      x = (1-t)*a + t*d
      x = _as_prob(x) # sum1
      ms = _scaled_max(x)
      if ms < best_ms - 1e-15:
          best_ms=ms; best=x
   return best,best_ms
```
Since x sum maybe 1, `_as_prob` may clip. It will not change much. But if all clipped? no.

Can we speed line search by using `np.dot(1-t*a+t*d, ...)`? no.

Potential issue: `_scaled_max` uses `_conv_full` direct; for many line searches, direct `np.convolve` may allocate large arrays each time. Fine.

Active LP implementation:
```
def _active_lp(a, act, n, lp_timeout=None):
    act = np.asarray(act, dtype=int)
    act = act[(act>=0)&(act<2*n-1)]
    if act.size==0: return None
    m=act.size
    A = np.zeros((m,n))
    nn=n
    ainv = n*a
    for r,k in enumerate(act):
       s = max(0, k-n+1); e = min(n, k+1)
       idx = np.arange(s,e)
       A[r, s:e] = a[k - idx] # not scaled? We'll scale later? Use scaled row directly: n*a
       # A row entries scaled: n*a[...]
```
Use scaled row directly to avoid multiply: `A[r,s:e] = n*a[k-idx]`.
```

```
Perturb function:
```
def _perturb(base, rng, sigma):
   n=base.size
   a=base + 1e-12
   z=np.log(a)
   z += rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
   # optionally incorporate small sinusoidal global mode to alter shape
   # z -= z.mean()
   # soft clip
   z -= np.max(z)
   w=np.exp(z)
   s=w.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return base.copy()
   return w/s
```
If sigma large, may create zeros but sum ok.
```

def _make_variants(n, parent, rng):
   if parent and parent.size==n:
       p=parent
       yield p
       yield p[::-1]
       z=np.log(p)+rng.normal(0,0.2,n); yield softmax
       # mix with shapes
       t=(arange(n)+0.5)/n; x=2t-1
       for shape in [tri, cos, two_bump]:
          shape=_as_prob(shape); yield (0.6*p+0.4*shape)
       # select random support: p**gamma? normalize
       for gamma in [2,0.5]: yield p**gamma normalized
   else:
       yield shape variants

import numpy as np

```
Helper `_is_prob`:
```
def _to_prob(arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)/arr.size
    return arr/s
```

`_conv_full`:
```
def _conv_full(a):
    n=a.size
    if n <= 512:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    fs=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    F=rfft(a, fs)
    b=irfft(F*F, fs)
    return b[:2*n-1]
```
Potential if n huge, FFT memory. We'll cap jobs ≤1024/2048. For n>2048, maybe use random? Not.

`_scaled_max`:
```
def _scaled_max(a):
    b=_conv_full(a)
    m=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m): return math.inf
    return float(a.size)*m
```

`_line_search`:
```
def _line_search(a,d,cur_ms,deadline):
    n=a.size
    if not np.isfinite(cur_ms): return a,cur_ms
    if n <= 300:
       ts=(1.0,0.8,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.3,0.25,0.2,0.15,0.1,0.075,0.05,0.03,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001)
    else:
       ts=(1.0,0.8,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.3,0.2,0.15,0.1,0.06,0.03,0.012,0.005,0.001)
    best=a; best_ms=cur_ms
    for t in ts:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 0.4: break
        x = (1.0 - t)*a + t*d
        x = _to_prob(x)
        ms = _scaled_max(x)
        if np.isfinite(ms) and ms < best_ms - 1e-15:
            best_ms = ms; best = x
    return best,best_ms
```
Using `_to_prob` each t may clip sum; if x already sum1 but with tiny negative from mixing? a,d nonneg sum1, so x nonneg sum1. `_to_prob` normalizes; may add small overhead. We can optimize: `s=x.sum(); if s>0: x=x/s; x[x<0]=0`. Use `_to_prob`.

`_build_active_A(a, act)`:
```
def _build_active_A(a, act):
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    act=np.asarray(act).ravel()
    act=act[(act>=0)&(act<L)]
    if act.size==0: return None, None
    A=np.zeros((act.size,n), dtype=np.float64)
    na = n*a
    for r,k in enumerate(act):
        k=int(k)
        s = max(0, k-n+1)
        e = min(n, k+1)
        idx = np.arange(s,e)
        A[r,s:e] = na[k-idx]
    return A, act
```
Where `na = n*a`.

`_active_lp`:
```
def _active_lp(a, act, deadline=None):
    A, act = _build_active_A(a, act)
    if A is None: return None, math.inf
    n=a.size; m=A.shape[0]
    if m == 0: return None, math.inf
    if m == 1:
        row=A[0]
        mn=float(row.min())
        mask=row <= mn + 1e-12*max(1.0, abs(mn))
        if not mask.any(): mask[0]=True
        vals=a[mask]
        sv=float(vals.sum())
        h=np.zeros(n); 
        if sv > 1e-15: h[mask]=vals/sv
        else: h[mask]=1.0/mask.sum()
        u=float(row @ h)
        return h,u
    if linprog is None:
        return None, math.inf
    c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0
    A_ub=np.empty((m,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_ub[:,:n]=A
    A_ub[:,n]=-1.0
    b_ub=np.zeros(m)
    A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1), dtype=np.float64); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
    b_eq=np.ones(1)
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n + [(0.0,None)]
    try:
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
    except TypeError:
       try:
          res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
       except Exception:
          res=None
    except Exception:
       res=None
    if res is None or res.x is None or res.status not in (0,2): # 1? maybe accept if x feasible
        # try old simplex if Res status 1 due to limit?
        if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            # If solver stopped, test feasibility
            pass
        else:
            try:
               res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='simplex')
            except Exception:
               return None, math.inf
    if res is None or res.x is None:
        return None, math.inf
    h=np.asarray(res.x[:-1], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    h=np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,None)
    s=float(h.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None, math.inf
    h/=s
    # Optionally enforce active feasibility by projecting? If u too high, maybe skip.
    vals=A@h
    u=float(np.max(vals))
    # If due to solver status not optimal, check feasible: if u <= a@A? Actually active self max maybe. Need current_ms? Not in function. We can return; caller checks u<cur_ms.
    return h,u

def _choose_active(bS, cur_ms, K, margin, target_ms):
    L=bS.size
    margin = max(1e-10, margin)
    thresh = max(cur_ms - margin, target_ms - margin)
    # We want include top K at least, and all above thresh if count <= K
    above = np.flatnonzero(bS >= thresh)
    if above.size > K:
        # top K among above? If target threshold includes many, top K highest.
        # But if some above threshold are not top? top K values by sorting bS. Use argsort.

    else:

```
Variant generation code:
```
def _shape_base(n, v, rng):
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n; x=2*t-1
    if v==0: a=np.ones(n)
    elif v==1: a=1-np.abs(x)
    elif v==2: a=1-x*x
    elif v==3: a=0.5*(1+np.cos(np.pi*x))
    elif v==4: a=np.exp(-3*abs(x))
    elif v==5: a=np.exp(-6*(t-0.3)**2)
    elif v==6: a=np.exp(-6*(t-0.7)**2)
    elif v==7: a=np.exp(-8*(t-0.25)**2)+np.exp(-8*(t-0.75)**2)
    elif v==8: a=0.5*(1+np.sin(2*np.pi*t))
    else: a=np.abs(rng.normal(0,1,n))+0.2
    return _to_prob(a)

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=float(budget_s)
    if total < 5: ... simple
    hard = t0 + max(0.0,total) - 4.0

    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    parent=_get_parent()
    if parent is not None:
       best_a=_to_prob(parent)
       best_score=2*_scaled_max(best_a)
    else:
       n0=192
       best_a=_shape_base(n0,0,np.random.default_rng(seed))
       best_score=2*_scaled_max(best_a)
    # exact parent? maybe
    try:
       fs=evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in best_a])
       if np.isfinite(fs) and fs < best_score: best_score=fs
    except: pass
    # jobs list
    jobs=[]
    n_p = best_a.size
    # job1 parent
    jobs.append((best_a.copy(), 0.45))
    # variants
    var1 = _combine(parent, _shape_base(n_p,1,rng), 0.7)
    jobs.append((var1,0.10))
    var2 = _perturb(best_a, rng, 0.25)
    jobs.append((var2,0.10))
    var3 = _combine(parent, _shape_base(n_p,7,rng), 0.5)
    jobs.append((var3,0.05))
    # other lengths
    other_set=[]
    for mult in [2/3,4/3,2,8/3,4/5,5/4]:
       n2=int(round(n_p*mult))
       if 64<=n2<=2048 and n2!=n_p and n2 not in other_set:
          other_set.append(n2)
    # sort maybe by closeness to n_p? take first 4
    for n2 in sorted(other_set, key=lambda n:abs(n-n_p))[:3]:
       init=_resample(parent,n2) if parent is not None else _shape_base(n2,1,rng)
       jobs.append((init,0.04))
    # final job best (remaining)
    # schedule
    usable=max(1.0, hard - t0)
    cur_time=t0
    allocations=[]
    rem=usable
    for a,frac in jobs:
       if cur_time >= hard-1: break
       frac_time = min(rem, max(1.0, usrable*frac))
       end=cur_time+frac_time
       if end > hard-1: end=hard-1
       allocations.append((a,end))
       cur_time=end
    # final endless
    # loop allocations
    for a_init,end in allocations:
       if time.monotonic() >= hard - 1.5: break
       res_a,res_ms = _optimize(a_init, min(end,hard), rng.copy(), max_stag=80)
       if res_ms*2 < best_score - 1e-12:
           cand=_to_prob(res_a); fs=2*res_ms; # exact maybe
           if np.isfinite(fs): best_score=min(best_score, fs? exact maybe)
           best_a=cand
    # final polish
    if time.monotonic() < hard - 2.0:
       res_a,res_ms=_optimize(best_a.copy(), hard, rng.copy(), max_stag=200)
       update
    # final exact
    cand_arr=_to_prob(best_a)
    if cand_arr.size==0 or not finite: cand_arr=np.ones(1)
    cand=[float(v) for v in cand_arr]
    try:
       fs=evaluate_sequence(cand)
    except: fs=math.inf
    if not np.isfinite(fs):
       return [1.0]
    return cand

def _combine(a,b,alpha):
   a=_to_prob(a); b=_to_prob(b)
   if a.size != b.size:
      # resample b to a.size? only if sizes differ
   return _to_prob(alpha*a + (1-alpha)*b)

```
Detailed `_optimize` code:
```
def _optimize(a, deadline, rng, max_stag=80):
    a=_to_prob(a)
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_ms=_scaled_max(a)
    cur=a.copy(); cur_ms=best_ms
    L=2*n-1
    if n <= 256: maxK=L
    elif n <= 512: maxK=min(L,160)
    else: maxK=min(L,80)
    K=min(16,maxK)
    margin_frac=1e-5
    status=0
    pert_count=0
    no_imp=0
    stag=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.4:
       # If cur invalid reset
       if not np.isfinite(cur_ms):
           cur=best.copy(); cur_ms=best_ms
       bS_vals = _conv_full(cur)
       cur_ms = float(n * np.max(bS_vals))
       if not np.isfinite(cur_ms): cur=best.copy(); cur_ms=best_ms; continue
       bS = n * bS_vals
       if cur_ms < best_ms - 1e-12:
           best=cur.copy(); best_ms=cur_ms; stag=0
       else:
           stag += 1
           if stag > max_stag: break
       # if time short and local best good break? If cur_ms close best? no.
       margin=max(1e-10, margin_frac*max(cur_ms, TARGET_MS))
       if TARGET_MS < cur_ms:
           thresh=TARGET_MS - margin
       else:
           thresh=cur_ms - margin
       mask = bS >= thresh
       cnt = int(mask.sum())
       if cnt == 0:
           act_arr = np.array([int(np.argmax(bS))])
       else:
           # near max count
           near = bS >= cur_ms - max(1e-8, 1e-7*cur_ms)
           cnt_near = int(near.sum())
           K_eff = max(K, min(maxK, cnt_near))
           if cnt <= K_eff:
               act_arr = np.flatnonzero(mask)
           else:
               # ensure we include all near max if possible
               # take top K_eff
               act_arr = np.argsort(bS)[::-1][:K_eff]
               # add any near max not included? top includes highest, so yes.
       if act_arr.size < 3:
           top = np.argsort(bS)[::-1][:min(3,L)]
           act_arr = np.unique(np.concatenate((act_arr, top)))
       # optional cap to maxK after adding
       if act_arr.size > maxK:
           act_arr = np.argsort(bS)[::-1][:maxK]
       h,u = _active_lp(cur, act_arr)
       if h is None:
           K = min(maxK, K*2+1)
           margin = min(1e-3, margin*3)
           no_imp += 1
           if no_imp >= 4:
               # perturb
               cur = _perturb(best, rng, min(0.8, 0.15*no_imp)); cur_ms=_scaled_max(cur)
               K=min(16,maxK); margin=...
               no_imp=1; pert_count+=1; stag=0
           continue
       # line search with mixed direction
       mix = 0.2 if u < cur_ms - 1e-8 else 0.0
       d = (1-mix)*h + mix*cur
       d = _to_prob(d)
       new,new_ms = _line_search(cur,d,cur_ms,deadline)
       if new_ms >= cur_ms - 1e-12 and mix>0:
           new,new_ms = _line_search(cur,h,cur_ms,deadline)
       if np.isfinite(new_ms) and new_ms < cur_ms - 1e-12:
           cur = new; cur_ms = new_ms; no_imp=0
           # maybe if improvement very small and K small, no.
       else:
           no_imp +=1
           K = min(maxK, max(K*2, 16))
           margin = min(1e-2, max(margin*4, 1e-6*max(cur_ms,TARGET_MS)))
           if no_imp >= 3:
               sigma = 0.12 + 0.08*min(6,pert_count)
               cur = _perturb(best, rng, min(1.0, sigma))
               cur_ms = _scaled_max(cur)
               K = min(24, maxK)
               margin = max(1e-7, 1e-4*max(cur_ms,TARGET_MS))
               no_imp = 1
               pert_count +=1
               stag = 0
               if pert_count >= 10:
                   break
    return best,best_ms

Now, exact best update function in main:
```
def update_best(a):
   nonlocal best_a,best_score
   arr=_to_prob(a)
   if arr.size==0: return
   fast=2*_scaled_max(arr)
   if not finite: return
   # only exact if fast could beat
   if fast < best_score + 1e-9:
       try: fs=evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in arr])
       except: fs=fast
       if finite fs and fs < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=fs; best_a=arr.copy()
```

```
def _fallback_dir(a,A):
    n=a.size; m=A.shape[0]
    best_h=a.copy(); best_mu=float(np.max(A@best_h))
    # Try p uniform and p = rows' max/norm
    rp = A.max(axis=1)
    p2 = rp/(rp.sum()+1e-300)
    for p in (np.ones(m)/m, p2):
        g = A.T @ p
        base = g - float(np.dot(a,g))
        # log-space step
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

TARGET_SCORE = 1.50274349
TARGET_MS = 0.5 * TARGET_SCORE
_FS_CACHE = {}


def _fft_len(n):
    if n not in _FS_CACHE:
        _FS_CACHE[n] = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    return _FS_CACHE[n]


def _to_prob(arr):
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def _conv_full(a):
    n = a.size
    if n <= 512:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    fs = _fft_len(n)
    F = rfft(a, fs)
    return irfft(F * F, fs)[: (2 * n - 1)]


def _scaled_max(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    b = _conv_full(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return float(a.size) * m


def _parent_array():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.sum() <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return p / p.sum()


def _resample(arr, n):
    a = _to_prob(arr)
    m = a.size
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, a[0] / n, dtype=np.float64)
    old = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / m
    new = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n
    y = np.interp(new, old, a)
    return _to_prob(y)


def _shape_base(n, variant, rng):
    t = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    v = int(variant) % 12
    if v == 0:
        a = np.ones(n)
    elif v == 1:
        a = 1.0 - np.abs(x)
    elif v == 2:
        a = 1.0 - x * x
    elif v == 3:
        a = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(np.pi * x))
    elif v == 4:
        a = np.exp(-3.0 * np.abs(x))
    elif v == 5:
        a = np.exp(-6.0 * (t - 0.30) ** 2)
    elif v == 6:
        a = np.exp(-6.0 * (t - 0.70) ** 2)
    elif v == 7:
        a = np.exp(-8.0 * (t - 0.25) ** 2) + np.exp(-8.0 * (t - 0.75) ** 2)
    elif v == 8:
        a = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t))
    elif v == 9:
        a = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * t))
    elif v == 10:
        a = np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * t))
    else:
        a = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)) + 0.2
    a = a + 1e-6 * np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
    return _to_prob(a)


def _combine(a, b, alpha):
    a = _to_prob(a)
    b = _to_prob(b)
    if a.size != b.size:
        b = _resample(b, a.size)
    return _to_prob(alpha * a + (1.0 - alpha) * b)


def _perturb(base, rng, sigma):
    a = _to_prob(base)
    a = a + 1e-12
    z = np.log(a)
    z = z + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, a.size)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    w = np.exp(np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0))
    return _to_prob(w)


def _random_prob(n, rng):
    a = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)) + 0.15
    a = _to_prob(a)
    b = _shape_base(n, int(rng.integers(0, 11)), rng)
    return _to_prob(0.5 * a + 0.5 * b)


def _line_search(a, d, cur_ms, deadline):
    n = a.size
    if n <= 300:
        ts = (
            1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.25, 0.2, 0.15,
            0.1, 0.075, 0.05, 0.03, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002,
            0.001, 0.0005
        )
    else:
        ts = (
            1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.15,
            0.1, 0.06, 0.03, 0.012, 0.005, 0.001
        )
    best = a
    best_ms = cur_ms
    for t in ts:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 0.35:
            break
        x = (1.0 - t) * a + t * d
        x = _to_prob(x)
        ms = _scaled_max(x)
        if np.isfinite(ms) and ms < best_ms - 1e-15:
            best_ms = ms
            best = x
    return best, best_ms


def _build_active_A(a, act):
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    act = np.asarray(act, dtype=int).ravel()
    act = act[(act >= 0) & (act < L)]
    if act.size == 0:
        return None
    A = np.zeros((act.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
    na = n * a
    for r, k in enumerate(act):
        k = int(k)
        s = max(0, k - n + 1)
        e = min(n, k + 1)
        if e <= s:
            continue
        idx = np.arange(s, e)
        A[r, s:e] = na[k - idx]
    return A


def _fallback_dir(a, A):
    n = a.size
    m = A.shape[0]
    if m == 0:
        return a.copy(), math.inf
    best_h = a.copy()
    best_mu = float(np.max(A @ best_h))
    p1 = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / m
    rowmax = np.max(A, axis=1)
    srm = float(rowmax.sum())
    if srm > 1e-300:
        p2 = rowmax / srm
    else:
        p2 = p1
    for p in (p1, p2):
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)) or p.sum() <= 1e-300:
            continue
        g = A.T @ p
        base = g - float(np.dot(a, g))
        gn = float(np.max(np.abs(base))) if base.size else 0.0
        if gn < 1e-18:
            continue
        z = np.log(a + 1e-12) - 0.6 * (base / gn)
        z = z - np.max(z)
        h = np.exp(np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0))
        h = _to_prob(h)
        mu = float(np.max(A @ h))
        if np.isfinite(mu) and mu < best_mu:
            best_h = h
            best_mu = mu
    return best_h, best_mu


def _active_lp(a, act):
    A = _build_active_A(a, act)
    if A is None:
        return None, math.inf
    n = A.shape[1]
    m = A.shape[0]
    if m == 0:
        return None, math.inf

    if m == 1:
        row = A[0]
        mn = float(row.min())
        tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(mn))
        mask = row <= mn + tol
        if not mask.any():
            mask[0] = True
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        vals = a[mask]
        sv = float(vals.sum())
        if sv > 1e-15:
            h[mask] = vals / sv
        else:
            h[mask] = 1.0 / float(mask.sum())
        u = float(row @ h)
        if not np.isfinite(u) or not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            return None, math.inf
        return h, u

    h_found = None
    if linprog is not None:
        c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c[n] = 1.0
        A_ub = np.empty((m, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_ub[:, :n] = A
        A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
        b_ub = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, None)]
        attempts = []
        try:
            attempts.append(("highs", {"presolve": True}))
        except Exception:
            pass
        attempts.append(("highs", {}))
        attempts.append(("simplex", {}))
        for meth, opts in attempts:
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    c,
                    A_ub=A_ub,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    A_eq=A_eq,
                    b_eq=b_eq,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method=meth,
                    options=opts
                )
            except Exception:
                continue
            if res is None or res.x is None:
                continue
            x = np.asarray(res.x[:-1], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
            sx = float(x.sum())
            if sx <= 1e-15:
                continue
            x = x / sx
            if A @ x is None:
                continue
            u = float(np.max(A @ x))
            if np.isfinite(u):
                h_found = x
                return x, u

    if h_found is None:
        h, u = _fallback_dir(a, A)
        if h is not None and np.isfinite(u):
            return h, u
    return None, math.inf


def _optimize(a, deadline, rng, max_stag=90):
    a = _to_prob(a)
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_ms = _scaled_max(a)
    cur = a.copy()
    cur_ms = best_ms
    if not np.isfinite(best_ms):
        best = np.ones(n) / n
        best_ms = _scaled_max(best)
        cur = best.copy()
        cur_ms = best_ms

    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 256:
        maxK = L
    elif n <= 512:
        maxK = min(L, 180)
    else:
        maxK = min(L, 96)

    K = min(18, maxK)
    margin = 1e-5
    no_imp = 0
    tiny = 0
    pert_count = 0
    stag = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.4:
        if not np.isfinite(cur_ms):
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_ms = best_ms

        b = _conv_full(cur)
        if b.size == 0:
            break
        cur_ms = float(n * np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(cur_ms):
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_ms = best_ms
            break

        bS = n * b

        if cur_ms < best_ms - 1e-12:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_ms = cur_ms
            stag = 0
        else:
            stag += 1
            if stag > max_stag:
                break

        marg = max(1e-10, margin * max(cur_ms, TARGET_MS, 1e-3))
        if TARGET_MS < cur_ms:
            thresh = TARGET_MS - marg
        else:
            thresh = cur_ms - marg

        mask = bS >= thresh
        cnt = int(mask.sum())
        if cnt == 0:
            act_arr = np.array([int(np.argmax(bS))], dtype=int)
        else:
            near = bS >= (cur_ms - max(1e-8, 1e-7 * cur_ms))
            cnt_near = int(near.sum())
            K_eff = max(K, min(maxK, cnt_near))
            if cnt <= K_eff:
                act_arr = np.flatnonzero(mask)
            else:
                act_arr = np.argsort(bS)[::-1][:K_eff]
            if act_arr.size < 3:
                top = np.argsort(bS)[::-1][:min(3, L)]
                act_arr = np.unique(np.concatenate((act_arr, top)))
            if act_arr.size > maxK:
                act_arr = np.argsort(bS)[::-1][:maxK]

        h, u = _active_lp(cur, act_arr)
        if h is None or not np.isfinite(u):
            K = min(maxK, 2 * K + 1)
            margin = min(1e-2, margin * 4.0 + 1e-7)
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 4:
                sigma = 0.12 + 0.08 * min(6, pert_count)
                cur = _perturb(best, rng, min(1.0, sigma))
                cur_ms = _scaled_max(cur)
                K = min(20, maxK)
                margin = 1e-5
                no_imp = 1
                pert_count += 1
                stag = 0
                tiny = 0
                if pert_count >= 12:
                    break
            continue

        if u < cur_ms - 1e-10:
            new, new_ms = _line_search(cur, h, cur_ms, deadline)
        else:
            new, new_ms = cur, cur_ms

        if np.isfinite(new_ms) and new_ms < cur_ms - 1e-12:
            dec = cur_ms - new_ms
            cur = new
            cur_ms = new_ms
            no_imp = 0
            if dec < 1e-7:
                tiny += 1
            else:
                tiny = 0
            if tiny > 35:
                break
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            K = min(maxK, max(2 * K, 24))
            margin = min(1e-2, max(margin * 4.0, 1e-5 * max(cur_ms, TARGET_MS)))
            if no_imp >= 3:
                sigma = 0.10 + 0.07 * min(7, pert_count)
                cur = _perturb(best, rng, min(1.0, sigma))
                cur_ms = _scaled_max(cur)
                K = min(24, maxK)
                margin = 1e-5
                no_imp = 1
                pert_count += 1
                stag = 0
                tiny = 0
                if pert_count >= 12:
                    break

    return best, best_ms


def _exact_eval(lst):
    try:
        ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        if ev is None:
            raise RuntimeError("no evaluator")
        return float(ev(lst))
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        total = 1300.0
    hard = t0 + total - 4.0
    if hard <= t0 + 1.0:
        hard = t0 + max(0.5, total - 0.5)

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    parent = _parent_array()
    if parent is not None:
        n_main = parent.size
        best_a = parent.copy()
    else:
        n_main = 192
        best_a = _shape_base(n_main, 0, rng)
    best_a = _to_prob(best_a)
    best_score_fast = 2.0 * _scaled_max(best_a)
    best_score = _exact_eval([float(v) for v in best_a])
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float(best_score_fast)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(1)
        best_score = 2.0

    def _update(a):
        nonlocal best_a, best_score
        a = _to_prob(a)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        fs_fast = 2.0 * _scaled_max(a)
        if not np.isfinite(fs_fast):
            return
        if fs_fast < best_score + 1e-9:
            fs = _exact_eval([float(v) for v in a])
            if np.isfinite(fs) and fs < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = fs
                best_a = a.copy()

    usable = max(1.0, hard - t0)

    if usable < 35.0:
        if parent is not None:
            res, _ = _optimize(parent.copy(), hard, rng, max_stag=40)
            _update(res)
        cand = _to_prob(best_a)
        fs = _exact_eval([float(v) for v in cand])
        if not np.isfinite(fs) or fs > 2.0:
            return [1.0]
        return [float(v) for v in cand]

    jobs = []
    if parent is not None:
        jobs.append((parent.copy(), 0.40))
        jobs.append((_perturb(parent, rng, 0.18), 0.09))
        jobs.append((_combine(parent, _shape_base(n_main, 1, rng), 0.70), 0.07))
        jobs.append((_combine(parent, _shape_base(n_main, 7, rng), 0.55), 0.06))
    else:
        jobs.append( (_shape_base(n_main, 0, rng), 0.25) )
        jobs.append( (_shape_base(n_main, 1, rng), 0.15) )
        jobs.append( (_random_prob(n_main, rng), 0.15) )

    jobs.append((_random_prob(n_main, rng), 0.05))
    jobs.append((_shape_base(n_main, 9, rng), 0.05))

    seen = {n_main}
    other_ns = []
    for mult in (2.0 / 3.0, 4.0 / 5.0, 4.0 / 3.0, 2.0, 8.0 / 3.0, 1.6, 1.2):
        nn = int(round(n_main * mult))
        if 64 <= nn <= 2048 and nn not in seen:
            seen.add(nn)
            other_ns.append(nn)
    other_ns.sort(key=lambda x: abs(x - n_main))
    for nn in other_ns[:3]:
        if parent is not None:
            init = _resample(parent, nn)
        else:
            init = _shape_base(nn, 1, rng)
        jobs.append((init, 0.04))

    scheduled = t0
    plan = []
    for a0, frac in jobs:
        if scheduled >= hard - 2.0:
            break
        d = max(2.0, frac * usable)
        end = min(hard - 1.0, scheduled + d)
        if end - scheduled < 1.0:
            continue
        plan.append((a0, end))
        scheduled = end

    for a0, end in plan:
        if time.monotonic() >= end - 0.5:
            continue
        try:
            res, _ = _optimize(a0, min(end, hard), rng.copy(), max_stag=80)
            _update(res)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.monotonic() < hard - 3.0:
        try:
            res, _ = _optimize(best_a.copy(), hard, rng.copy(), max_stag=250)
            _update(res)
        except Exception:
            pass

    cand = _to_prob(best_a)
    cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
    cs = float(cand.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(cs) or cs <= 0.01:
        cand = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        cand = cand / cs

    fs = _exact_eval([float(v) for v in cand])
    if not np.isfinite(fs):
        if parent is not None:
            pc = _to_prob(parent)
            fs2 = _exact_eval([float(v) for v in pc])
            if np.isfinite(fs2):
                return [float(v) for v in pc]
        return [1.0]

    return [float(v) for v in cand]
```
